=== battle 1
rules: Platinum rules
format: Single
mine:
  mine#0 Ponyta L66 Male Relaxed Run Away @nothing | Agility, Fire Blast, Bounce, Flare Blitz | HP 143 Atk 127 Def 106 SpA 104 SpD 99 Spe 116
  mine#1 Magnezone L6 Genderless Mild Sturdy @Soul Dew | Metal Sound, Barrier, Mirror Coat, Thunder Shock | HP 25 Atk 14 Def 17 SpA 23 SpD 17 Spe 13
  mine#2 Aipom L40 Male Modest Run Away @nothing | Agility, Double Hit, Fling, Nasty Plot | HP 99 Atk 55 Def 49 SpA 53 SpD 53 Spe 82
foe: Ace Trainer Tester, ai Basic, items none
  foe#0 Silcoon L45 Male Bashful Shed Skin @nothing | Harden | HP 109 Atk 49 Def 60 SpA 40 SpD 36 Spe 27
  foe#1 Delcatty L24 Female Lonely Normalize @nothing | Double Slap, Sing, Attract, Fake Out | HP 72 Atk 39 Def 37 SpA 31 SpD 36 Spe 40
  foe#2 Deoxys L46 Genderless Careful Pressure @Silver Powder | Teleport, Knock Off, Pursuit, Psychic | HP 106 Atk 147 Def 56 SpA 128 SpD 67 Spe 153
log:
  Seen Species=Silcoon
  TrainersStand First=Ace Trainer Second=none
  say Ace Trainer Tester wants to battle!
  say Ace Trainer Tester sent out Silcoon!
    + Entered E0 Pokemon=foe#0 Silcoon FromBall=true
  say Go! Ponyta!
    + Entered P0 Pokemon=mine#0 Ponyta FromBall=true
  say Ponyta sprang up!
    + Lunged P0 Category=Status
    + Vanished P0
  say Foe Silcoon used Harden!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Status
    + MoveShown Move=Harden Type=Normal Category=Status From=E0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Foe Silcoon's Defense rose!
    + StageChanged E0 Stat=Defense Stage=1 Rose=true
  say Ponyta used Bounce!
    + Reappeared P0
    + Lunged P0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Bounce Type=Flying Category=Physical From=P0 To=E0 Missed=true Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Ponyta's attack missed!
  say Foe Silcoon used Harden!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Status
    + MoveShown Move=Harden Type=Normal Category=Status From=E0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Foe Silcoon's Defense rose!
    + StageChanged E0 Stat=Defense Stage=2 Rose=true
  say Ponyta used Fire Blast!
    + Lunged P0 Category=Special
    + MoveShown Move=Fire Blast Type=Fire Category=Special From=P0 To=E0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=true
    ! Struck E0 Hp=0 Hard=true
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=true NotVeryEffective=false
  say It's super effective!
  say Foe Silcoon fainted!
    + Fainted E0
    + TrainerReacts E0 Cue=FirstDown
  say Ponyta gained 693 EXP. Points!
  ExpGained Pokemon=mine#0 Ponyta Amount=693
  say Ace Trainer Tester sent out Deoxys!
    + Seen Species=Deoxys
    + Entered E0 Pokemon=foe#2 Deoxys FromBall=true
  say Foe Deoxys is exerting its Pressure!
    + AbilityShown E0 Ability=Pressure
  say Foe Deoxys used Pursuit!
    + Lunged E0 Category=Physical
    + MoveShown Move=Pursuit Type=Dark Category=Physical From=E0 To=P0 Missed=false Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
    ! Struck P0 Hp=119 Hard=false
    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
  say Ponyta used Fire Blast!
    + Lunged P0 Category=Special
    + MoveShown Move=Fire Blast Type=Fire Category=Special From=P0 To=E0 Missed=true Blocked=false Critical=false SuperEffective=false
  say Ponyta's attack missed!
  say Foe Deoxys used Pursuit!

rules: Platinum rules
format: Double
mine:

  mine#2 Combusken L74 Male Bold Blaze @nothing | Slash, Mirror Move, Sky Uppercut, Flare Blitz | HP 183 Atk 128 Def 114 SpA 144 SpD 111 Spe 90

  foe#0 Primeape L22 Male Lonely Anger Point @Protector | Fury Swipes, Karate Chop, Seismic Toss, Screech | HP 66 Atk 60 Def 31 SpA 32 SpD 36 Spe 49
  foe#1 Tangrowth L32 Female Lonely Leaf Guard @nothing | Vine Whip, Bind, Mega Drain, Stun Spore | HP 108 Atk 84 Def 81 SpA 78 SpD 40 Spe 45
  foe#2 Chimchar L10 Female Impish Blaze @Icicle Plate | Leer, Scratch, Ember, Taunt | HP 30 Atk 17 Def 14 SpA 16 SpD 13 Spe 18

  Seen Species=Primeape
  Seen Species=Tangrowth
  TrainersStand First=Ace Trainer Second=none
  say Ace Trainer Tester wants to battle!
  say Ace Trainer Tester sent out Primeape and Tangrowth!

    ! HitSounded SuperEffective=false NotVeryEffective=false
